and [TLBI RVAALE1IS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-33-TLBI-RVAALE1IS--TLBI-RVAALE1ISNXS--TLB-Range-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_rvaale1is).
      - If [FEAT\_TLBIOS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-5-The-Armv8-4-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_tlbios) and [FEAT\_TLBIRANGE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-5-The-Armv8-4-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_tlbirange) are implemented, [TLBI RVAE1OS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-37-TLBI-RVAE1OS--TLBI-RVAE1OSNXS--TLB-Range-Invalidate-by-VA--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_rvae1os), [TLBI RVAAE1OS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-31-TLBI-RVAAE1OS--TLBI-RVAAE1OSNXS--TLB-Range-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_rvaae1os), [TLBI RVALE1OS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-46-TLBI-RVALE1OS--TLBI-RVALE1OSNXS--TLB-Range-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_rvale1os), and [TLBI RVAALE1OS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance/-C5-5-34-TLBI-RVAALE1OS--TLBI-RVAALE1OSNXS--TLB-Range-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-Level--EL1--Outer-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_tlbi_rvaale1os).
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, then the following instructions are trapped to EL2 and reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - [TLBIALLIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-140-TLBIALLIS--TLB-Invalidate-All--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiallis), [TLBIMVAIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-156-TLBIMVAIS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvais), [TLBIASIDIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-144-TLBIASIDIS--TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiasidis), [TLBIMVAAIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-151-TLBIMVAAIS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaais), [TLBIMVALIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-160-TLBIMVALIS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvalis), and [TLBIMVAALIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-153-TLBIMVAALIS--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-level--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaalis).
      - [TLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-137-TLBIALL--TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiall), [TLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-149-TLBIMVA--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimva), [TLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-143-TLBIASID--TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiasid), [TLBIMVAA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-150-TLBIMVAA--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaa), [TLBIMVAL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-157-TLBIMVAL--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimval), and [TLBIMVAAL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-152-TLBIMVAAL--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-level?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaal)
      - [ITLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-105-ITLBIALL--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbiall), [ITLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-107-ITLBIMVA--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbimva), and [ITLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-106-ITLBIASID--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbiasid).
      - [DTLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-48-DTLBIALL--Data-TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbiall), [DTLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-50-DTLBIMVA--Data-TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbimva), and [DTLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-49-DTLBIASID--Data-TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbiasid).

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TTLB
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     EL1 execution of the specified TLB maintenance instructions are trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    > #### Note
    >
    > The TLB maintenance instructions are UNDEFINED at EL0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TPU, bit [24]
:   Trap cache maintenance instructions that operate to the Point of Unification. Traps execution of those cache maintenance instructions at EL0 and EL1 to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, as follows:

    - If EL0 is using AArch64 and the value of [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCI is 1, then the following instructions at EL0 are trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x18`:
      - [IC IVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau) and [DC CVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-29-DC-CVAU--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvau).
    - If EL1 is using AArch64, then the following instructions at EL1 are trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x18`:
      - [IC IVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-41-IC-IVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ivau), [IC IALLU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-39-IC-IALLU--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_iallu), [IC IALLUIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-40-IC-IALLUIS--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ialluis), and [DC CVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-29-DC-CVAU--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvau).
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, then the following instructions are trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - [ICIMVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-82-ICIMVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_icimvau), [ICIALLU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-80-ICIALLU--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_iciallu), [ICIALLUIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-81-ICIALLUIS--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_icialluis), and [DCCMVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-42-DCCMVAU--Data-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccmvau).

    > #### Note
    >
    > When [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCI is 0, the trap on execution of instructions at EL0 is higher priority than this control.
    >
    > An exception generated because an instruction is UNDEFINED at EL0 is higher priority than this trap to EL2. In addition:
    >
    > - [IC IALLUIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-40-IC-IALLUIS--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_ialluis) and [IC IALLU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-39-IC-IALLU--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ic_iallu) are always UNDEFINED at EL0 using AArch64.
    > - [ICIMVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-82-ICIMVAU--Instruction-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_icimvau), [ICIALLU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-80-ICIALLU--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_iciallu), [ICIALLUIS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-81-ICIALLUIS--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU--Inner-Shareable?lang=en#reg_aarch32_icialluis), and [DCCMVAU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-42-DCCMVAU--Data-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccmvau) are always UNDEFINED at EL0 using AArch32.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TPU
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     For each of the specified instructions, if the execution of the instruction can be trapped, access is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TPCP, bit [23]
:   Trap data or unified cache maintenance instructions that operate to the Point of Coherency, Persistence, or Physical Storage. When EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, traps execution of cache maintenance instructions at EL0 and EL1 to EL2 as follows:

    - If EL0 is using AArch64 and the value of [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCI is 1, then the following instructions at EL0 are trapped to EL2 and reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`:
      - [DC CIVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-21-DC-CIVAC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_civac) and [DC CVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-25-DC-CVAC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvac).
      - If [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) is implemented, [DC CIGVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-17-DC-CIGVAC--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigvac), [DC CIGDVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-13-DC-CIGDVAC--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigdvac), [DC CGVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-7-DC-CGVAC--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgvac), and [DC CGDVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-2-DC-CGDVAC--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvac).
      - If [FEAT\_DPB](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-3-The-Armv8-2-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb) is implemented, [DC CVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-28-DC-CVAP--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvap).
      - If [FEAT\_DPB](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-3-The-Armv8-2-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb) and [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) are implemented, [DC CGVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-9-DC-CGVAP--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgvap) and [DC CGDVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-5-DC-CGDVAP--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvap).
      - If [FEAT\_DPB2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb2) is implemented, [DC CVADP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-26-DC-CVADP--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoDP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvadp).

      - If [FEAT\_DPB](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-3-The-Armv8-2-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb) is implemented, [DC CVAP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-28-DC-CVAP--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvap).

      - If [FEAT\_DPB2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb2) is implemented, [DC CVADP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-26-DC-CVADP--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoDP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvadp).
      - If [FEAT\_DPB2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_dpb2) and [FEAT\_MTE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte) are implemented, [DC CGVADP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-8-DC-CGVADP--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoDP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgvadp) and [DC CGDVADP](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-3-DC-CGDVADP--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoDP?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvadp).

      - If [FEAT\_PoPS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-7-The-Armv9-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_pops) and [FEAT\_MTE2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte2) are implemented, [DC CIGDVAPS](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-15-DC-CIGDVAPS--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-PoPS?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigdvaps).
    - If [FEAT\_OCCMO](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-7-The-Armv9-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_occmo) is implemented, [DC CIVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-22-DC-CIVAOC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_civaoc), [DC CIGDVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-14-DC-CIGDVAOC--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigdvaoc), [DC CVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-27-DC-CVAOC--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cvaoc) and [DC CGDVAOC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-4-DC-CGDVAOC--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-VA-to-Outer-Cache-level?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdvaoc).
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, then the following instructions at EL1 are trapped to EL2 and reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - [DCIMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-44-DCIMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dcimvac), [DCCIMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-39-DCCIMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccimvac), and [DCCMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-41-DCCMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccmvac).

    > #### Note
    >
    > This field was previously named TPC.
    >
    > When [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCI is 0, the trap on execution of instructions at EL0 is higher priority than this control.
    >
    > An exception generated because an instruction is UNDEFINED at EL0 is higher priority than this trap to EL2. In addition:
    >
    > - AArch64 instructions which invalidate by VA to the Point of Coherency or Physical Storage are always UNDEFINED at EL0 using AArch64.
    > - [DCIMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-44-DCIMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dcimvac), [DCCIMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-39-DCCIMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccimvac), and [DCCMVAC](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-41-DCCMVAC--Data-Cache-line-Clean-by-VA-to-PoC?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccmvac) are always UNDEFINED at EL0 using AArch32.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TPCP
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     For each of the specified instructions, if the execution of the instruction can be trapped, it is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TSW, bit [22]
:   Trap data or unified cache maintenance instructions that operate by Set/Way. Traps execution of those cache maintenance instructions at EL1 to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, as follows:

    - If EL1 is using AArch64, then the following instructions are trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x18`:
      - [DC ISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-36-DC-ISW--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_isw), [DC CSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-24-DC-CSW--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_csw), and [DC CISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-20-DC-CISW--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cisw).
      - If [FEAT\_MTE2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-6-The-Armv8-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_mte2) is implemented, [DC IGSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-34-DC-IGSW--Invalidate-of-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_igsw), [DC IGDSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-32-DC-IGDSW--Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_igdsw), [DC CGSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-6-DC-CGSW--Clean-of-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgsw), [DC CGDW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-1-DC-CGDSW--Clean-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cgdsw), [DC CIGSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-16-DC-CIGSW--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigsw), and [DC CIGDSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-12-DC-CIGDSW--Clean-and-Invalidate-of-Data-and-Allocation-Tags-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cigdsw).
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, then the following instructions are trapped to EL2 and reported with EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - [DCISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-45-DCISW--Data-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dcisw), [DCCSW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-43-DCCSW--Data-Cache-line-Clean-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccsw), and [DCCISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-40-DCCISW--Data-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccisw).

    > #### Note
    >
    > An exception generated because an instruction is UNDEFINED at EL0 is higher priority than this trap to EL2, and these instructions are always UNDEFINED at EL0.

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TACR, bit [21]
:   Trap Auxiliary Control Registers. Traps EL1 accesses to the Auxiliary Control Registers to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, as follows:

    - If EL1 is using AArch64, accesses to [ACTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-2-ACTLR-EL1--Auxiliary-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_actlr_el1) to EL2, are trapped to EL2 and reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, accesses to [ACTLR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-1-ACTLR--Auxiliary-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_actlr) and, if implemented, [ACTLR2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-2-ACTLR2--Auxiliary-Control-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch32_actlr2) are trapped to EL2 and reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TACR
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     EL1 accesses to the specified registers are trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>
   </td>

 </tbody>
</table>

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    > #### Note
    >
    > [ACTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-2-ACTLR-EL1--Auxiliary-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_actlr_el1) is not accessible at EL0.
    >
    > [ACTLR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-1-ACTLR--Auxiliary-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_actlr) and [ACTLR2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-2-ACTLR2--Auxiliary-Control-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch32_actlr2) are not accessible at EL0.
    >
    > The Auxiliary Control Registers are IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED registers that might implement global control bits for the PE.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TIDCP, bit [20]
:   Trap IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED functionality. Traps EL1 accesses to the encodings reserved for IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED functionality to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state as follows:

    - In AArch64 state, EL1 access to the encodings in the following reserved encoding spaces are trapped:
      - IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED System instructions, which are accessed using SYS and SYSL, with CRn == {11, 15}, and are reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
      - IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED System instructions, which are accessed using SYSP, with CRn == {11, 15}, and are reported using EC syndrome value `0x14`.
      - IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED System registers, which are accessed using MRS and MSR with the [S3\_<op1>\_<Cn>\_<Cm>\_<op2>](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-167-S3--op1---Cn---Cm---op2---IMPLEMENTATION-DEFINED-Registers?lang=en#reg_aarch64_s3_op1_cn_cm_op2) register name, and are reported using EC syndrome `0x18`.
      - IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED System registers, which are accessed using MRRS and MSRR with the [S3\_<op1>\_<Cn>\_<Cm>\_<op2>](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-167-S3--op1---Cn---Cm---op2---IMPLEMENTATION-DEFINED-Registers?lang=en#reg_aarch64_s3_op1_cn_cm_op2) register name, and are reported using EC syndrome `0x14`.
    - In AArch32 state, MCR and MRC access to instructions with the following encodings are trapped and reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - All coproc==p15, CRn==c9, opc1 == {0-7}, CRm == {c0-c2, c5-c8}, opc2 == {0-7}.
      - All coproc==p15, CRn==c10, opc1 =={0-7}, CRm == {c0, c1, c4, c8}, opc2 == {0-7}.
      - All coproc==p15, CRn==c11, opc1=={0-7}, CRm == {c0-c8, c15}, opc2 == {0-7}.

    For accesses to these regions of encoding space by EL0, all of the following apply:

    - If FEAT\_TIDCP1 is implemented, traps caused by [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).TIDCP and [SCTLR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-175-SCTLR-EL2--System-Control-Register--EL2-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el2).TIDCP are reported with higher priority than traps caused by this control.
    - If FEAT\_TIDCP1 is not implemented, or the access is not trapped by SCTLR\_ELx.TIDCP, one of the following applies:
      - If [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).TIDCP is 0 then it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED, for each encoding, whether the access is performed, is UNDEFINED, or is trapped by an IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED mechanism.
      - If [HCR\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-61-HCR-EL2--Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcr_el2).TIDCP is 1 then it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED, for each encoding, whether the access is trapped to EL2. If it is not trapped it is UNDEFINED.

 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TIDCP
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>

 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     EL1 accesses to or execution of the specified encodings reserved for
     <small>
      IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED
     </small>
     functionality are trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>

    An implementation can also include IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED registers that provide additional controls, to give finer-grained control of the trapping of IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED features.

    > #### Note
    >
    > The trapping of accesses to these registers from EL1 is higher priority than an exception resulting from the register access being UNDEFINED.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TSC, bit [19]
:   Trap `SMC` instructions. Traps EL1 execution of `SMC` instructions to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.

    If execution is in AArch64 state, the trap is reported using EC syndrome value `0x17`.

    If execution is in AArch32 state, the trap is reported using EC syndrome value `0x13`.

    > #### Note
    >
    > HCR\_EL2.TSC traps execution of the `SMC` instruction. It is not a routing control for the `SMC` exception. Trap exceptions and `SMC` exceptions have different preferred return addresses.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TSC
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     If EL3 is implemented, then any attempt to execute an
     <code>
      SMC
     </code>
     instruction at EL1 is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, regardless of the value of
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
      SCR_EL3
     </a>
     .SMD.
    </p>
    <p>
     If EL3 is not implemented and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.NV is 1, then any attempt to execute an SMC instruction at EL1 using AArch64 is trapped to EL2.
    </p>
    <p>
     If EL3 is not implemented and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.NV is 0, then it is
     <small>
      IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED
     </small>
     whether:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Any attempt to execute an SMC instruction at EL1 is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Any attempt to execute an SMC instruction is
       <small>
        UNDEFINED
       </small>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    In AArch32 state, the Armv8-A architecture permits, but does not require, this trap to apply to conditional `SMC` instructions that fail their condition code check, in the same way as with traps on other conditional instructions.

    `SMC` instructions are UNDEFINED at EL0.

    If EL3 is not implemented and the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.NV is 0, then it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether this bit is:

    - RES0.
    - Implemented with the functionality as described in HCR\_EL2.TSC.

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TID3, bit [18]
:   Trap ID group 3. Traps EL1 reads of group 3 ID registers to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, as follows:

    In AArch64 state:

    - Reads of the following registers are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`:
      -       - If [FEAT\_FGT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-7-The-Armv8-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_fgt) is implemented, reads of the following registers are trapped to EL2.         - [ID\_PFR2\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-115-ID-PFR2-EL1--AArch32-Processor-Feature-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_pfr2_el1), [ID\_MMFR4\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-111-ID-MMFR4-EL1--AArch32-Memory-Model-Feature-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_mmfr4_el1) and [ID\_MMFR5\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-112-ID-MMFR5-EL1--AArch32-Memory-Model-Feature-Register-5?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_mmfr5_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64MMFR3\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-90-ID-AA64MMFR3-EL1--AArch64-Memory-Model-Feature-Register-3?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64mmfr3_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64MMFR4\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-91-ID-AA64MMFR4-EL1--AArch64-Memory-Model-Feature-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64mmfr4_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64PFR2\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-94-ID-AA64PFR2-EL1--AArch64-Processor-Feature-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64pfr2_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64MMFR2\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-89-ID-AA64MMFR2-EL1--AArch64-Memory-Model-Feature-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64mmfr2_el1) and [ID\_ISAR6\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-106-ID-ISAR6-EL1--AArch32-Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-6?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_isar6_el1).
        - [ID\_DFR1\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-99-ID-DFR1-EL1--AArch32-Debug-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_dfr1_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64ZFR0\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-96-ID-AA64ZFR0-EL1--SVE-Feature-ID-Register-0?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64zfr0_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64SMFR0\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-95-ID-AA64SMFR0-EL1--SME-Feature-ID-Register-0?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64smfr0_el1).
        - [ID\_AA64ISAR2\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-85-ID-AA64ISAR2-EL1--AArch64-Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch64_id_aa64isar2_el1).
      - If [FEAT\_FGT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-7-The-Armv8-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_fgt) is implemented, this field traps all MRS accesses to registers in the following range that are not already mentioned in this field description: op0 == 3, op1 == 0, CRn == 0, CRm == {2-7}, op2 == {0-7}. If FEAT\_FGT is not implemented, it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether this field traps accesses to registers in the range.

    In AArch32 state:

    - VMRS access to [MVFR0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-116-MVFR0--Media-and-VFP-Feature-Register-0?lang=en#reg_aarch32_mvfr0), [MVFR1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-117-MVFR1--Media-and-VFP-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch32_mvfr1), and [MVFR2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-118-MVFR2--Media-and-VFP-Feature-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch32_mvfr2), are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x08`, unless access is also trapped by [HCPTR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-64-HCPTR--Hyp-Architectural-Feature-Trap-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_hcptr) which takes priority.
    - MRC access to the following registers are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`:
      - [ID\_PFR0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-99-ID-PFR0--Processor-Feature-Register-0?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_pfr0), [ID\_MMFR1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-94-ID-MMFR1--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr1), [ID\_MMFR2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-95-ID-MMFR2--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr2), [ID\_MMFR3](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-96-ID-MMFR3--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-3?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr3), [ID\_ISAR3](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-89-ID-ISAR3--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-3?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar3), [ID\_ISAR4](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-90-ID-ISAR4--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar4), and [ID\_ISAR5](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-91-ID-ISAR5--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-5?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar5).
      - If [FEAT\_FGT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-7-The-Armv8-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_fgt) is implemented:
        - [ID\_MMFR4](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-97-ID-MMFR4--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr4) and [ID\_MMFR5](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-98-ID-MMFR5--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-5?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr5).
        - [ID\_ISAR6](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-92-ID-ISAR6--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-6?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar6).
        - [ID\_DFR1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-85-ID-DFR1--Debug-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_dfr1).
        - This field traps all MRC accesses to encodings in the following range that are not already mentioned in this field description: coproc == p15, opc1 == 0, CRn == c0, CRm == {c2-c7}, opc2 == {0-7}.
      - If [FEAT\_FGT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-7-The-Armv8-6-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_fgt) is not implemented:
        - [ID\_MMFR4](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-97-ID-MMFR4--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr4) and [ID\_MMFR5](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-98-ID-MMFR5--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-5?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr5) are trapped to EL2, unless implemented as RAZ, when it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether accesses to [ID\_MMFR4](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-97-ID-MMFR4--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-4?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr4) or [ID\_MMFR5](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-98-ID-MMFR5--Memory-Model-Feature-Register-5?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_mmfr5) are trapped.
        - [ID\_ISAR6](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-92-ID-ISAR6--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-6?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar6) is trapped to EL2, unless implemented as RAZ, when it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether accesses to [ID\_ISAR6](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-92-ID-ISAR6--Instruction-Set-Attribute-Register-6?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_isar6) are trapped to EL2.
        - [ID\_DFR1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-85-ID-DFR1--Debug-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_dfr1) is trapped to EL2, unless implemented as RAZ, when it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether accesses to [ID\_DFR1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-85-ID-DFR1--Debug-Feature-Register-1?lang=en#reg_aarch32_id_dfr1) are trapped to EL2.
        - Otherwise, it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether this bit traps all MRC accesses to registers in the following range not already mentioned in this field description with coproc == p15, opc1 == 0, CRn == c0, CRm == {c2-c7}, opc2 == {0-7}.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TID3
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     The specified EL1 read accesses to ID group 3 registers are trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TID2, bit [17]
:   Trap ID group 2. Traps the following register accesses to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, as follows:

    - If EL1 is using AArch64, reads of [CTR\_EL0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-41-CTR-EL0--Cache-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ctr_el0), [CCSIDR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-31-CCSIDR-EL1--Current-Cache-Size-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ccsidr_el1), [CCSIDR2\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-30-CCSIDR2-EL1--Current-Cache-Size-ID-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ccsidr2_el1), [CLIDR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-32-CLIDR-EL1--Cache-Level-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_clidr_el1), and [CSSELR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-40-CSSELR-EL1--Cache-Size-Selection-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_csselr_el1) are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
    - If EL0 is using AArch64 and the value of [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCT is not 0, reads of [CTR\_EL0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-41-CTR-EL0--Cache-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ctr_el0) are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`. If the value of [SCTLR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1).UCT is 0, then EL0 reads of [CTR\_EL0](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-41-CTR-EL0--Cache-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_ctr_el0) are trapped to EL1 and the resulting exception takes precedence over this trap.
    - If EL1 is using AArch64, writes to [CSSELR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-40-CSSELR-EL1--Cache-Size-Selection-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_csselr_el1) are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, reads of [CTR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-37-CTR--Cache-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_ctr), [CCSIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-24-CCSIDR--Current-Cache-Size-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_ccsidr), [CCSIDR2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-25-CCSIDR2--Current-Cache-Size-ID-Register-2?lang=en#reg_aarch32_ccsidr2), [CLIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-27-CLIDR--Cache-Level-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_clidr), and [CSSELR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-36-CSSELR--Cache-Size-Selection-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_csselr) are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`.
    - If EL1 is using AArch32, writes to [CSSELR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-36-CSSELR--Cache-Size-Selection-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_csselr) are trapped to EL2, reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TID1, bit [16]
:   Trap ID group 1. Traps EL1 reads of the following registers to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state as follows:

    - In AArch64 state, accesses of [REVIDR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-155-REVIDR-EL1--Revision-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_revidr_el1) and [AIDR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-13-AIDR-EL1--Auxiliary-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_aidr_el1), reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
    - When [FEAT\_SME](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-3-The-Armv9-2-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_sme) is implemented, accesses of [SMIDR\_EL1](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-186-SMIDR-EL1--Streaming-Mode-Identification-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_smidr_el1), reported using EC syndrome value `0x18`.
    - In AArch32 state, accesses of [TCMTR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-136-TCMTR--TCM-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tcmtr), [TLBTR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-161-TLBTR--TLB-Type-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbtr), [REVIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-123-REVIDR--Revision-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_revidr), and [AIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-4-AIDR--Auxiliary-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_aidr), reported using EC syndrome value `0x03`.

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TID0, bit [15]
:   When FEAT\_AA32 is implemented:
    :   Trap ID group 0. Traps the following register accesses to EL2:

        - EL1 reads of the [JIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-108-JIDR--Jazelle-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_jidr), reported using EC syndrome value `0x05`.
        - If the [JIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-108-JIDR--Jazelle-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_jidr) is RAZ from EL0, EL0 reads of the [JIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-108-JIDR--Jazelle-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_jidr), reported using EC syndrome value `0x05`.
        - EL1 accesses using VMRS of the [FPSID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-56-FPSID--Floating-Point-System-ID-register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_fpsid), reported using EC syndrome value `0x08`.

        > #### Note
        >
        > - It is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether the [JIDR](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-108-JIDR--Jazelle-ID-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_jidr) is RAZ or UNDEFINED at EL0. If it is UNDEFINED at EL0, then any resulting exception takes precedence over this trap.
        > - The [FPSID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-56-FPSID--Floating-Point-System-ID-register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_fpsid) is not accessible at EL0 using AArch32.
        > - Writes to the [FPSID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-56-FPSID--Floating-Point-System-ID-register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_fpsid) are ignored, and not trapped by this control.

        When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

        The reset behavior of this field is:

        - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

    Otherwise:

TWE, bit [14]
:   Traps EL0 and EL1 execution of WFE instructions to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, from both Execution states, reported using EC syndrome value `0x01`.

    When [FEAT\_WFxT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_wfxt) is implemented, this trap also applies to the WFET instruction.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     TWE
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Any attempt to execute a WFE instruction at EL1 or EL0 is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, if the instruction would otherwise have caused the PE to enter a low-power state and it is not trapped by
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr">
      SCTLR
     </a>
     .nTWE or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1">
      SCTLR_EL1
     </a>
     .nTWE.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    In AArch32 state, the attempted execution of a conditional WFE instruction is trapped only if the instruction passes its condition code check.

    > #### Note
    >
    > Since a WFE can complete at any time, even without a Wakeup event, the traps on WFE are not guaranteed to be taken, even if the WFE is executed when there is no Wakeup event. The only guarantee is that if the instruction does not complete in finite time in the absence of a Wakeup event, the trap will be taken.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    For more information about when WFE instructions can cause the PE to enter a low-power state, see [‘Wait for Event mechanism and Send event’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-7-Mechanisms-for-entering-a-low-power-state/-D1-7-1-Wait-for-Event?lang=en#beijhbbd).

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

TWI, bit [13]
:   Traps EL0 and EL1 execution of WFI instructions to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, from both Execution states, reported using EC syndrome value `0x01`.

    When [FEAT\_WFxT](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-8-The-Armv8-7-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_wfxt) is implemented, this trap also applies to the WFIT instruction.

     This control does not cause any instructions to be trapped.
    </p>

  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Any attempt to execute a WFI instruction at EL0 or EL1 is trapped to EL2, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, if the instruction would otherwise have caused the PE to enter a low-power state and it is not trapped by
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr">
      SCTLR
     </a>
     .nTWI or
     <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1">
      SCTLR_EL1
     </a>
     .nTWI.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>

    In AArch32 state, the attempted execution of a conditional WFI instruction is trapped only if the instruction passes its condition code check.

    > #### Note
    >
    > Since a WFI can complete at any time, even without a Wakeup event, the traps on WFI are not guaranteed to be taken, even if the WFI is executed when there is no Wakeup event. The only guarantee is that if the instruction does not complete in finite time in the absence of a Wakeup event, the trap will be taken.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    For more information about when WFI instructions can cause the PE to enter a low-power state, see [‘Wait for Interrupt’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-7-Mechanisms-for-entering-a-low-power-state/-D1-7-2-Wait-for-Interrupt-mechanism?lang=en#beijbejd).

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

DC, bit [12]
:   Default Cacheability.

 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
