 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control has no effect on the EL1&amp;0 translation regime.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     In any Security state:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       When EL1 is using AArch64, the PE behaves as if the value of the
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1">
        SCTLR_EL1
       </a>
       .M field is 0 for all purposes other than returning the value of a direct read of
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-174-SCTLR-EL1--System-Control-Register--EL1-?lang=en#reg_aarch64_sctlr_el1">
        SCTLR_EL1
       </a>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       When EL1 is using AArch32, the PE behaves as if the value of the
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr">
        SCTLR
       </a>
       .M field is 0 for all purposes other than returning the value of a direct read of
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-127-SCTLR--System-Control-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch32_sctlr">
        SCTLR
       </a>
       .
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       The PE behaves as if the value of the HCR_EL2.VM field is 1 for all purposes other than returning the value of a direct read of HCR_EL2.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       The memory type produced by stage 1 of the EL10 translation regime is Normal Non-Shareable, Inner Write-Back Read-Allocate Write-Allocate, Outer Write-Back Read-Allocate Write-Allocate.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    This field has no effect on the EL2, EL2&0, and EL3 translation regimes.

    This bit is permitted to be cached in a TLB.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

BSU, bits [11:10]
:   Barrier Shareability upgrade. This field determines the minimum shareability domain that is applied to any barrier instruction executed from EL1 or EL0:

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     BSU
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b00
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     No effect.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b01
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Inner Shareable.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b10
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Outer Shareable.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b11
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Full system.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    This value is combined with the specified level of the barrier held in its instruction, using the same principles as combining the shareability attributes from two stages of address translation.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

FB, bit [9]
:   Force broadcast. Causes the following Non-shareable invalidate instructions to be broadcast within the Inner Shareable domain when executed from EL1:

    - In AArch32 state: [BPIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-21-BPIALL--Branch-Predictor-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_bpiall), [TLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-137-TLBIALL--TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiall), [TLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-149-TLBIMVA--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimva), [TLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-143-TLBIASID--TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbiasid), [DTLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-48-DTLBIALL--Data-TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbiall), [DTLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-50-DTLBIMVA--Data-TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbimva), [DTLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-49-DTLBIASID--Data-TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dtlbiasid), [ITLBIALL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-105-ITLBIALL--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-All?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbiall), [ITLBIMVA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-107-ITLBIMVA--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-by-VA?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbimva), [ITLBIASID](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-106-ITLBIASID--Instruction-TLB-Invalidate-by-ASID-match?lang=en#reg_aarch32_itlbiasid), [TLBIMVAA](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-150-TLBIMVAA--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaa), [ICIALLU](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-80-ICIALLU--Instruction-Cache-Invalidate-All-to-PoU?lang=en#reg_aarch32_iciallu), [TLBIMVAL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-157-TLBIMVAL--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--Last-level?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimval), and [TLBIMVAAL](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-152-TLBIMVAAL--TLB-Invalidate-by-VA--All-ASID--Last-level?lang=en#reg_aarch32_tlbimvaal).
    - In AArch64 state:
      - All `TLBI` instructions that are executable at EL1 and do not specify IS or OS.
      - All `TLBIP` instructions that are executable at EL1 and do not specify IS or OS.
      - `IC IALLU`.

    For more information, see [‘A64 System instructions for TLB maintenance’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-5-A64-System-instructions-for-TLB-maintenance?lang=en#aarch64_operations_tlb_maintenance).

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     FB
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     The specified instructions are not affected by this control.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When one of the specified Non-shareable instructions is executed at EL1, the operation is broadcast within the Inner Shareable shareability domain.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

VSE, bit [8]
:   Virtual SError exception.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     VSE
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This mechanism is not making a virtual SError exception pending.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     A virtual SError exception is pending because of this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The virtual SError exception is enabled only when HCR\_EL2.TGE is 0 and either HCR\_EL2.AMO is 1 or [FEAT\_DoubleFault2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-2-Armv8-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-2-10-The-Armv8-9-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_doublefault2) is implemented and the Effective value of [HCRX\_EL2](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-62-HCRX-EL2--Extended-Hypervisor-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_hcrx_el2).TMEA is 1.

    When [FEAT\_E3DSE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse) is implemented, virtual SError exceptions pended by this field have priority over delegated SError exceptions pended by [SCR\_EL3](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3).DSE.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

VI, bit [7]
:   Virtual IRQ Interrupt.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     VI
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This mechanism is not making a virtual IRQ pending.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     A virtual IRQ is pending because of this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The virtual IRQ is enabled only when the value of HCR\_EL2.{TGE, IMO} is {0, 1}.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

VF, bit [6]
:   Virtual FIQ Interrupt.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     VF
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This mechanism is not making a virtual FIQ pending.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     A virtual FIQ is pending because of this mechanism.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    The virtual FIQ is enabled only when the value of HCR\_EL2.{TGE, FMO} is {0, 1}.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

AMO, bit [5]
:   Physical SError exception routing.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     AMO
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3 and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       The routing of physical SError exceptions is unaffected by this mechanism.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       If
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse">
        FEAT_E3DSE
       </a>
       is implemented, then delegated SError exceptions enabled by
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
        SCR_EL3
       </a>
       .DSE are not taken to EL2.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual SError exceptions are not enabled by this mechanism.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3, EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Physical SError exceptions are taken to EL2, unless they are routed to EL3.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       If
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse">
        FEAT_E3DSE
       </a>
       is implemented, then delegated SError exceptions enabled by
       <a class="document-topic" document-topic-path="/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3" href="/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D24-AArch64-System-Register-Descriptions/-D24-2-General-system-control-registers/-D24-2-168-SCR-EL3--Secure-Configuration-Register?lang=en#reg_aarch64_scr_el3">
        SCR_EL3
       </a>
       .DSE are taken to EL2.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual SError exceptions are enabled.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When executing at EL3, the value of HCR\_EL2.AMO has no impact on the behavior of the PE.

    When executing at Exception levels below EL3, and EL2 is not enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.AMO is 0.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, regardless of the value of the AMO bit, all of the following are true:

    - Physical SError exceptions target EL2 unless they are routed to EL3.
    - If [FEAT\_E3DSE](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-A-Arm-Architecture-Introduction-and-Overview/-Chapter-A2-A-profile-Architecture-Extensions/-A2-3-Armv9-A-architecture-extensions/-A2-3-6-The-Armv9-5-architecture-extension?lang=en#feat_feat_e3dse) is implemented, delegated SError exceptions target EL2.
    - Virtual SError exceptions are disabled.

    When executing at EL2 and the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 0}, it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.AMO is 1 or the value programmed.

    For more information, see [‘Asynchronous exception routing’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-4-Exceptions/-D1-4-6-Asynchronous-exception-types?lang=en#beidhdhf).

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

IMO, bit [4]
:   Physical IRQ Routing.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     IMO
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3 and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Physical IRQ interrupts are not taken to EL2.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual IRQ interrupts are disabled.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3, EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Physical IRQ exceptions are taken to EL2, unless they are routed to EL3.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual IRQ interrupts are enabled.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When executing at EL3, the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.IMO has no impact on the behavior of the PE.

    When executing at Exception levels below EL3, and EL2 is not enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.IMO is 0.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, regardless of the value of the IMO bit, all of the following are true:

    - Physical IRQ Interrupts target EL2 unless they are routed to EL3.
    - Virtual IRQ interrupts are disabled.

    When executing at EL2 and the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 0}, it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.IMO is 1 or the value programmed.

    For more information, see [‘Asynchronous exception routing’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-4-Exceptions/-D1-4-6-Asynchronous-exception-types?lang=en#beidhdhf).

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

FMO, bit [3]
:   Physical FIQ Routing.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     FMO
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3 and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Physical FIQ interrupts are not taken to EL2.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual FIQ interrupts are disabled.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     When executing at Exception levels below EL3, EL2 is enabled in the current Security state, and the Effective value of HCR_EL2.TGE is 0:
    </p>
    <ul>
     <li>
      <p>
       Physical FIQ exceptions are taken to EL2, unless they are routed to EL3.
      </p>
     </li>
     <li>
      <p>
       Virtual FIQ interrupts are enabled.
      </p>
     </li>
    </ul>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When executing at EL3, the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.FMO has no impact on the behavior of the PE.

    When executing at Exception levels below EL3, and EL2 is not enabled in the current Security state, the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.FMO is 0.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, regardless of the value of the FMO bit, all of the following are true:

    - Physical FIQ Interrupts target EL2 unless they are routed to EL3.
    - Virtual FIQ interrupts are disabled.

    When executing at EL2 and the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 0}, it is IMPLEMENTATION DEFINED whether the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.FMO is 1 or the value programmed.

    For more information, see [‘Asynchronous exception routing’](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-D-The-AArch64-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-D1-The-AArch64-System-Level-Programmers--Model/-D1-4-Exceptions/-D1-4-6-Asynchronous-exception-types?lang=en#beidhdhf).

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

PTW, bit [2]
:   Protected Table Walk. In the EL1&0 translation regime, a translation table access made as part of a stage 1 translation table walk is subject to a stage 2 translation. The combining of the memory type attributes from the two stages of translation means the access might be made to a type of Device memory. If this occurs, then the value of this bit determines the behavior:

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     PTW
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     The translation table walk occurs as if it is to Normal Non-cacheable memory. This means it can be made speculatively.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     The memory access generates a stage 2 Permission fault.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    This bit is permitted to be cached in a TLB.

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

SWIO, bit [1]
:   Set/Way Invalidation Override. Causes EL1 execution of the data cache invalidate by set/way instructions to perform a data cache clean and invalidate by set/way:

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     SWIO
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     This control has no effect on the operation of data cache invalidate by set/way instructions.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     Data cache invalidate by set/way instructions perform a data cache clean and invalidate by set/way.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When the value of this bit is 1:

    AArch32: [DCISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-45-DCISW--Data-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dcisw) performs the same invalidation as a [DCCISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-G-The-AArch32-System-Level-Architecture/-Chapter-G8-AArch32-System-Register-Descriptions/-G8-2-General-system-control-registers/-G8-2-40-DCCISW--Data-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch32_dccisw) instruction.

    AArch64: [DC ISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-36-DC-ISW--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_isw) performs the same invalidation as a [DC CISW](/documentation/ddi0487/mc/-Part-C-The-AArch64-Instruction-Set/-Chapter-C5-The-A64-System-Instruction-Class/-C5-3-A64-System-instructions-for-cache-maintenance/-C5-3-20-DC-CISW--Data-or-unified-Cache-line-Clean-and-Invalidate-by-Set-Way?lang=en#reg_aarch64_dc_cisw) instruction.

    This bit can be implemented as RES1.

    When HCR\_EL2.TGE is 1, the PE ignores the value of this field for all purposes other than a direct read of this field.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

VM, bit [0]
:   Virtualization enable. Enables stage 2 address translation for the EL1&0 translation regime, when EL2 is enabled in the current Security state.

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     VM
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     Meaning
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     EL1&amp;0 stage 2 address translation disabled.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b1
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     EL1&amp;0 stage 2 address translation enabled.
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

    When the value of this bit is 1, data cache invalidate instructions executed at EL1 perform a data cache clean and invalidate. For the invalidate by set/way instruction this behavior applies regardless of the value of the HCR\_EL2.SWIO bit.

    This bit is permitted to be cached in a TLB.

    When the Effective value of HCR\_EL2.{E2H, TGE} is {1, 1}, the Effective value of this field is 0.

    The reset behavior of this field is:

    - On a Warm reset, this field resets to an architecturally UNKNOWN value.

###### Accessing HCR\_EL2

Accesses to this register use the following encodings in the System register encoding space:

###### `MRS <Xt>, HCR_EL2`

<table>
 <colgroup>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
  <col/>
 </colgroup>
 <thead>
  <tr>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op0
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op1
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     CRn
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     CRm
    </strong>
   </th>
   <th>
    <strong>
     op2
    </strong>
   </th>
  </tr>
 </thead>
 <tbody>
  <tr>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b11
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b100
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b0001
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
   <td>
    <p>
     <code>
      0b000
     </code>
    </p>
   </td>
  </tr>
 </tbody>
</table>

```
if !IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_AA64) then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL0 then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL1 then
    if EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'1x1'} then
        X{64}(t) = NVMem(0x078);
    elsif EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'xx1'} then
        AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL2, 0x18);
    else
        Undefined();
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL2 then
    X{64}(t) = HCR_EL2();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL3 then
    X{64}(t) = HCR_EL2();
end;
```

###### `MSR HCR_EL2, <Xt>`

    </strong>

     </code>
    </p>

```
if !IsFeatureImplemented(FEAT_AA64) then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL0 then
    Undefined();
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL1 then
    if EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'1x1'} then
        NVMem(0x078) = X{64}(t);
    elsif EffectiveHCR_EL2_NVx() IN {'xx1'} then
        AArch64_SystemAccessTrap(EL2, 0x18);
    else
        Undefined();
    end;
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL2 then
    HCR_EL2() = X{64}(t);
elsif PSTATE.EL == EL3 then
    HCR_EL2() = X{64}(t);
end;
```
